# Licenses: what each publisher's own page allows

Energy Research Warehouse (ERW), session 205, 12 October 2026. Written by `scripts/licenses.py write` from three files in `warehouse/metadata/` (`license_reads.csv`, `license_verdicts.csv`, `license_tables.csv`) and `coverage.csv`. Do not edit this file by hand.

- **What a verdict is.** A session read one page of each publisher, once, and took the sentence that allows or forbids redistribution. The verdict is that session's reading of the sentence. It is not legal advice, and no lawyer has read it.
- **Two verdicts a publisher.** "As the ERW is today": an open research project that sells nothing. "If the ERW sells anything": the same sentence read for a commercial product. They differ where a page allows noncommercial use only.
- **Nothing was changed in `coverage.csv`.** Where its flag and the verdict differ, the table is listed below and a person rules.
- **A publisher not read has no verdict.** A table that would otherwise be public is then "not settled", never public by default.
- **A quoted sentence is cut by code from the page as it was saved,** never typed. The pages themselves are a publisher's and stay on the data machine (`runs/license_reads/`).

## Read these first

- **609 tables.** The flag and the verdict agree on 499; a ruling settles 44 (session 324: the "license needed" rows of `license_status.csv`, and 2 tables kept internal by a ruling); disagree on 0; 65 are not settled (a publisher not read); 1 is internal by the ERW's own choice.
- **The ERW's verdict (session 378): 447 public, 112 internal, 50 unsettled** (one word a table, in "Every table"; an unsettled table waits for a permission, a read, or a ruling).
- **1582 publishers in the record: 1580 requests (each sweep under its own ceiling: session 205, 40; sessions 307 and 308, one each; session 311, 60; session 388, 16 with its robots files; sessions 484, 493 and 496, one each), 312 pages read, 1268 that could not be read, 2 not asked (paused).**
- **42 tables are flagged public where a publisher's sentence asks for permission or a license** (42 of them settled as "license needed"). By the publisher that decides it (a table can be under several): NYISO 31; ISO-NE 27; MISO 24; Bloomberg 4; Data Center Dynamics 4; Data Center Frontier 4; Financial Times 4; Hart Energy 4; Heatmap News 4; Latitude Media 4; Natural Gas Intelligence 4; OilPrice.com 4; POWER Magazine 4; Reuters 4; WSJ 4; Data Center Knowledge 3; Amazon Web Services 2; Digital Realty 2; Equinix 2; Google 2; Meta 2; Microsoft 2; Oracle 2; Switch 2; Vantage Data Centers 2; ANS Nuclear Newswire 2; Canary Media 2; JPT 2; LNG Prime 2; Rigzone 2; Utility Dive 2; pv magazine USA 2; CivicPlus 1; Hydrogen Insight 1; T&D World 1.
- **0 tables are flagged internal where the sentence read allows them.**
- **15 tables that may be public today would need permission if the ERW sells anything** (SPP 7; Canada Energy Regulator (CER) 2; Alberta Electric System Operator (AESO), Canada 1; California Energy Commission (CEC) 1; IMF 1; IMF PortWatch 1; Red Electrica (REE) 1; RRC 1).

## The publishers: the sentence and the verdict

| Publisher | Read | The sentence taken from the page | As the ERW is today | If the ERW sells anything | Who to write to | Price | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| EIA | #1: read | "U.S. government publications are in the public domain and are not subject to copyright protection. You may use and/or distribute any of our data, files, databases, reports, graphs, charts, and other information products that are on our website or that you receive through our email distribution service." | may be public | may be public | not named | none stated | An acknowledgment is asked for: "Source: U.S. Energy Information Administration" with the date. Items others contributed to EIA's site (NYMEX futures, ICE hub prices) are their owners'. |
| ERCOT | #2: read | "The publicly available contents of this website may be used, reproduced, and redistributed, provided that the contents are not modified and that you maintain all copyright and other notices contained in the contents, including this Agreement. Notwithstanding the foregoing, raw data provided in public portions of this website may be used, reproduced, and redistributed in compilations, charts, and analyses without maintaining such notices." | may be public | may be public | not named | none stated | Item 6 forbids use that harms the site's performance; it names no automated access as such. |
| CAISO | #3: read | "Most of the materials and information contained on this Website and the California ISO mobile application were generated, compiled or assembled from materials and information that are freely available for public use consistent with the general policies of the Public Records Act (California Government Code section 6250, et seq.), as provided by California Public Utilities Code section 345.5, and may be used by you provided that you keep intact all copyright, trademark and other proprietary notices and that you credit the California ISO when using such materials and/or information." | may be public | may be public | not named | none stated | Credit the California ISO and keep its notices. The same page's API terms forbid use "for any other benchmarking or competitive purposes". |
| ISO-NE | #4: read | "You are also hereby put on notice that the Content is protected by copyright under United States laws. Any duplication of the Content or non-personal use may violate copyright, trademark, and other laws. [...] To request permission to use a graph, chart, or other image on this website, please email info@iso‑ne.com" | needs written permission | needs written permission | info@iso-ne.com (the page names it for permission to use a graph, chart or image); Participant Support, (413) 540-4220 | none stated |  |
| NYISO | #5: read | "Access to this Web site does not confer any license or ownership interest in either the form or content of the Web site, including any confidential or proprietary information or intellectual property of any kind or nature, and the NYISO hereby expressly reserves such rights and property in its entirety." | needs written permission | needs written permission | none named on the page | none stated | The next sentence forbids republishing "any image or video on this website as a stand-alone file"; data are not named. Session 308 (the owner's default of 18 October 2026 to ruling 1 of docs/state/morning_2026-10-14.md): nyiso_as_prices gets no scheduled step until this license is answered. |
| SPP | #6: read | "Permission is implicitly granted to copy and distribute (via computer network or printed form) in whole or in part (with appropriate citation) EXCEPT when such materials will be used, in whole or in part, within a commercial publication (printed or otherwise) or when the author(s) or SPP will be quoted in commercial materials, forums or publications. Any commercial use of these materials requires prior, express written authorization from the author(s) or a duly authorized officer of SPP. ## Every table

"Decided by" names the publisher whose sentence sets the verdict. A table of the ERW's own work has no publisher.

"ERW verdict" (session 378) is one word a table: public (flagged public, and every publisher's sentence read allows it), internal (flagged internal), unsettled (a publisher not read, a "license needed" ruling waiting for its permission, or a flag and a verdict that disagree). A verdict that needs a document the ERW does not hold says so under "Not read"; such a table stays as it is flagged.

## Settled by a ruling (session 324)

The flag and the verdict differ, and a newer record says what holds. A table flagged public whose publisher asks for permission keeps its flag by the owner's ruling of 12 October 2026 (session 207): from the day in `warehouse/metadata/license_status.csv` it shows "license needed" in place of its numbers until a permission is recorded in its row. The verdict is right about the clause; the flag is the table's place once permitted.

## Held figures that still show (session 505)

A table held for a license is drawn as "license needed" (`site/lib/license.ts`, `components/Num.tsx`). `site/scripts/check-holds.mjs` reads every page of check-values' list on a build and lists each figure drawn from a held table without those words. How such a figure is held is Samuel's ruling; no page was changed. Read on 2026-10-21: 14 pages.

- `/digest`: 1 held figures shown (number), from `iso_rtm_hub_prices`.
- `/digest/2026-10-07`: 1 held figures shown (number), from `iso_rtm_hub_prices`.
- `/digest/2026-10-08`: 1 held figures shown (number), from `iso_rtm_hub_prices`.
- `/digest/2026-10-12`: 1 held figures shown (number), from `iso_rtm_hub_prices`.
- `/digest/2026-10-14`: 1 held figures shown (number), from `iso_rtm_hub_prices`.
- `/digest/2026-10-21`: 1 held figures shown (number), from `iso_rtm_hub_prices`.
- `/grid?grid=isone`: 36 held figures shown (number), from `isone_interconnection_queue`.
- `/grid/isone`: 36 held figures shown (number), from `isone_interconnection_queue`.
- `/research`: 4 held figures shown (panel), from `iso_hub_prices_history`, `iso_rtm_hub_prices`, `iso_zone_prices_history`, grids ISO-NE; NYISO.
- `/research?lunch_grid=isone`: 1 held figures shown (view), from `iso_hub_prices_history`, `iso_rtm_hub_prices`, `iso_zone_prices_history`, grids ISO-NE.
- `/research?lunch_grid=nyiso`: 1 held figures shown (view), from `iso_hub_prices_history`, `iso_rtm_hub_prices`, `iso_zone_prices_history`, grids NYISO.

## The publishers' prices, for the letter of intent (session 378)

A figure is written only where the page states one; none does. The four grid operators' letters are drafts under `docs/reviews/licenses/`, for Samuel to send.
